===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: }
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   8: import javax.sql.DataSource;
   9: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-122: bounded, non-locking committed ledger reads; no managed-position row locks.
  15:  * STOP_LOSS is the terminal reason written by executeSignalLinkedExit. The distinct
  16:  * POSITION_STOP_LOSS and partial-harvest reasons are deliberately not included.
  17:  */
  18: @Service
  19: public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
  20:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  21:     private final TransactionTemplate read;
  22:     private final TransactionTemplate write;
  23: 
  24:     public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  25:         jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
  26:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  27:         read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  28:         read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  29:         read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  30:         read.setReadOnly(true);
  31:         read.setTimeout(2);
  32:         write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  33:         write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  34:         write.setTimeout(2);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
  45:                 .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
  58:         return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
  59:     }
  60: 
  61:     public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
  62:         return jdbc.queryForList("""
  63:                 SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
  64:                 AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
  65:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
  66:     }
  67: }
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.java =====

  25: /**
  26:  * FIX-11Q ƒ?" Replay-only one-candle continuation grace experiment.
  27:  *
  28:  * The Production-parity Shadow position always remains authoritative. This observer starts a
  29:  * separate counterfactual position only after the existing PositionContinuationPolicy has already
  30:  * returned FAIL at a reached take-profit checkpoint. Existing continuation PASS paths therefore
  31:  * remain untouched and continue to extend take profit exactly as before.
  32:  *
  33:  * The counterfactual holds through one specific NEUTRAL 1m candle without moving TP. The grace is
  34:  * tied to that candle (not one live-price tick), may be consumed only once per position lifetime,
  35:  * and normal shared continuation/exit policies resume after a new 1m candle arrives.
  36:  */
  37: @Slf4j
  38: @Service
  39: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  40: public class OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver {
  41:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  42: 
  43:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  44:     private final PositionContinuationPolicy continuationPolicy;
  45:     private final PositionExitPolicy exitPolicy;
  46:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  47:     private final TradeExecutionValidationService executionValidationService;
  48:     private final WalletSettingsRepository walletSettingsRepository;
  49: 
  50:     private final Map<Long, Variant> activeByRun = new HashMap<>();
  51:     private final Map<Long, java.util.Set<Long>> consumedByRun = new HashMap<>();
  52: 
  53:     public synchronized void startRun(long runId, String symbol) {
  54:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
  55:         consumedByRun.put(runId, new java.util.HashSet<>());
  56:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_START runId={} symbol={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL productionMutation=false baselineReplayMutation=false", runId, symbol);
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     public synchronized void observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  60:             long runId, String symbol, long positionId, Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price,
  61:             Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal cost,
  62:             BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit, BigDecimal highest,
  63:             boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
  64:             int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
  65:             int entryMomentum, int entryVolume,
  66:             TradeSignal current1m, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  67:             String baselineExplanation) {
  68: 
  69:         if (activeByRun.containsKey(runId) || consumedByRun.getOrDefault(runId, java.util.Set.of()).contains(positionId)) return;
  70:         if (!eligible(current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour, entryMomentum)) return;
  71: 
  72:         Instant candleIdentity = candleIdentity(current1m);
  73:         if (candleIdentity == null) return;
  74:         BigDecimal baselinePnl = price.multiply(quantity, MC).subtract(cost, MC);
  75:         BigDecimal baselinePnlPct = percentage(entryPrice, price);
  76:         BigDecimal safeHighest = highest == null ? price : highest.max(price);
  77: 
  78:         Variant v = new Variant(positionId, symbol, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, stopLoss,
  79:                 takeProfit, safeHighest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
  80:                 entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  81:                 candleIdentity, current1m == null ? null : current1m.getId(), observedAt, price,
  82:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, price, price, current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  83:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
  84:         consumedByRun.computeIfAbsent(runId, k -> new java.util.HashSet<>()).add(positionId);
  85: 
  86:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  87:                 INSERT INTO one_candle_continuation_grace_test
  88:                 (test_run_id,position_test_id,symbol,grace_at,grace_1m_signal_id,grace_candle_time,
  89:                  price_at_grace,entry_price,take_profit_before_grace,take_profit_after_grace,
  90:                  current_1m_decision,five_minute_decision,one_hour_decision,trend_score,trend_floor,
  91:                  momentum_score,momentum_floor,baseline_action,baseline_exit_price,baseline_pnl_usdt,
  92:                  baseline_pnl_percent,status,baseline_explanation)
  93:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
  94:                 """, runId, positionId, symbol, Timestamp.from(observedAt), v.graceSignalId(), Timestamp.from(candleIdentity),
  95:                 price, entryPrice, takeProfit, takeProfit, name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()),
  96:                 name(oneHour.getDecision()), current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend),
  97:                 current1m.getMomentumScore(), momentumFloor(entryMomentum), "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", price,
  98:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, "ACTIVE", baselineExplanation);
  99: 
 100:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_ACTIVATED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} observedAt={} candle={} price={} tp={} current={} five={} one={} trend={}/{} momentum={}/{} action=HOLD_TARGET_UNCHANGED",
 101:                 runId, positionId, symbol, observedAt, candleIdentity, price, takeProfit,
 102:                 name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()), name(oneHour.getDecision()),
 103:                 current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend), current1m.getMomentumScore(), momentumFloor(entryMomentum));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public synchronized void onSignal(long runId, TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 107:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 108:         if (v == null || signal == null) return;
 109:         if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1mDecision() == null && !sameGraceCandle(v, signal)) {
 110:             v = v.withNext1m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next5mDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 113:             v = v.withNext5m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 114:         }
 115:         if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1hDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 116:             v = v.withNext1h(name(signal.getDecision()));
 117:         }
 118:         activeByRun.put(runId, v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h));
 119:     }
 120: 
 121:     public synchronized void onPrice(long runId, String symbol, MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 122:                                      TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 123:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 124:         if (v == null || event == null || event.price() == null || !v.symbol().equals(symbol)) return;
 125:         if (!event.observedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) return;
 126: 
 127:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 128:         v = v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h).withPath(price);
 129:         boolean sameGraceCandle = sameGraceCandle(v, latest1m);
 130: 
 131:         // Hard protection remains active even during grace; FIX-11Q suppresses only the baseline
 132:         // TAKE_PROFIT exit caused by transient continuation failure.
 133:         if (v.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(v.stopLoss()) <= 0) {
 134:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "STOP_LOSS", "Stop loss reached during/after grace."); return;
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         v = updateProfitLock(v, price);
 138:         if (v.profitLockActive() && v.profitLockPrice() != null && price.compareTo(v.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 139:             BigDecimal hardFloor = v.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 140:             if (price.compareTo(hardFloor) < 0) {
 141:                 close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", "Profit-lock hard floor breached in FIX-11Q counterfactual."); return;
 142:             }
 143:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lock = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 144:             if (lock.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, lock.code(), lock.explanation()); return; }
 145:         }
 146: 
 147:         if (v.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(v.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 148:             if (sameGraceCandle) {
 149:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v); // hold entire candle; target intentionally unchanged
 150:                 return;
 151:             }
 152:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 153:                     latest1m, latest5m, latest1h, v.entryTrend(), v.entryStructure(), v.entryMomentum(), v.entryVolume(),
 154:                     v.entryConfidence(), v.entryScore());
 155:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 156:                 BigDecimal distance = v.takeProfit().subtract(v.entryPrice());
 157:                 BigDecimal newTarget = v.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 158:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v.withTakeProfit(newTarget));
 159:                 log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_TP_EXTENDED runId={} positionId={} oldTp={} newTp={} reason={}", runId, v.positionId(), v.takeProfit(), newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 160:                 return;
 161:             }
 162:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation()); return;
 163:         }
 164: 
 165:         if (!sameGraceCandle) {
 166:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normal = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 167:             if (normal.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, normal.code(), normal.explanation()); return; }
 168:             if (latest1m != null && "1m".equals(latest1m.getInterval()) && bearish(latest1m.getDecision())) {
 169:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validated = executionValidationService.validateSell(latest1m);
 170:                 if (validated.allowed()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, validated.code(), validated.explanation()); return; }
 171:             }
 172:         }
 173:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
 174:     }
 175: 
 176:     public synchronized void finishRun(long runId, String symbol, Instant endTime) {
 177:         Variant v = activeByRun.remove(runId);
 178:         if (v != null) {
 179:             BigDecimal pnl = v.lastPrice().multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 180:             updateRow(runId, v, endTime, v.lastPrice(), "OPEN_AT_WINDOW_END", pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), v.lastPrice()));

 117:     @Transactional
 118:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecoveredForProcessing(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant at) {
 119:         TradeSignal signal = analyzeRecovered(indicator, at);
 120:         // This legacy LIVE-mode recovery route is execution-capable; it must not
 121:         // retain the historical-only marker used by non-executing catch-up rows.
 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");
 123:         signal = signalRepository.save(signal);
 124:         processingStore.register(signal, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.RECOVERY);
 125:         return signal;
 126:     }

 130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.
 131:      */
 132:     @Transactional
 133:     public TradeSignal analyze(String symbol, String interval) {
 134:         TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 135:                 .getLatest(symbol, interval)
 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));
 139: 
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }

 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {

 213:         if (profiler == null) {
 214:             log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_MISSING run={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false", runId);

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The preparation is not freeze-ready: P01–P07 are all pending, including exact baseline hashes, evidence E03–E10, canonical specification, approvals, async guard proof, and sealed acceptance criteria. [Preparation gates](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:53)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The required canonical specification remains a different, unfrozen `RPR-0.4-DRAFT`; the reviewed RPR-0.9.12 resides outside the workspace and its hash cannot substitute for the mandated canonical artifact. [Canonical draft](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:1)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The document requires test/log assertions against “Section 3.3.1,” but contains no such decision-precedence section. The implementation oracle for TP, stop, profit-lock, rebase, and near-TP ordering is therefore absent. [Unresolved references](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:152)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current Replay violates the desired boundary: it reads `wallet_settings`, invokes shared execution validation, uses wallet-shaped state, invokes live Binance minimum-order evaluation, queries `wallet_trade`, and writes shared diagnostics. The preparation accurately identifies these as target gaps, not completed protections. [Replay dependencies](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:45) [Live Binance path](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:687) [Shared diagnostics/account read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:37)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Equal-time price-event replay cannot currently be certified: SQL orders by `(observed_at,id)`, but `PriceEvent` retains neither ID nor delivery status; Replay then sorts only by timestamp. R156-06 correctly names the necessary future repair. [Projection](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118) [Replay sort](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:89)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The “zero Production code changes” boundary is not yet made enforceable. R156-05 names `ExecutionReplayScope`, which is injected into multiple Production-path services; its current thread-local mode also falls back to account evidence whenever no scope is active. The preparation does not identify a Replay-only replacement file graph that proves Production source remains untouched. [R156-05](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:72) [Thread-local scope](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:31) [Account fallback](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java:20)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The Replay-only objective is technically achievable, but only by adding a genuinely Replay-owned execution/evidence path and leaving all existing Production/shared-path source unchanged. The present preparation is not sufficiently specific to prove that constraint.

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The current working tree is materially dirty, so P01’s reproducible-baseline requirement is objectively unmet.
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `AnalysisService` uses a broad historical decision graph—sentiment, fundamentals, prior indicators, ATR, regime, strategy, market/BTC context, derivatives, order-book liquidity, and final decision. R156-04 specifies only limited policy and exchange-constraint fixtures, while the required full as-of input/refusal registry is not present in the preparation. [Analysis dependencies](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:73) [As-of calls](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:220) [R156-04](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:71)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Null regenerated signal IDs presently disable Replay consumption rather than establish a stable Replay-owned key. [Null behavior](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:84)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The narrow `fix122_evaluation` exception is owner-approved, and its current delete excludes Production rows (`test_run_id IS NULL`). However, it remains a shared-table operation requiring the promised exact allow-list and direct/async proof before freeze. [Exception](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FIX-156_IMPLEMENTATION_PREPARATION.md:84) [Current purge](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:360)

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Freeze must define “zero Production code changes” as a mechanically auditable rule: no edits to shared/Production-path classes, or a precise allow-list of Replay-only branches proven unreachable from Production. As written, R156-05 leaves this material boundary ambiguous.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not implement policy mirrors until every analysis-time input has a dated provenance, fingerprint, availability-time rule, and refusal/non-comparable outcome. Otherwise current configuration or late-arriving evidence can create look-ahead bias.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not freeze parity percentages until the confidence method, denominator, exclusion cap, cohort stratification, transaction-cost/slippage stance, and sealed acceptance period are stated before acceptance data is opened. P07 correctly recognizes this but remains pending.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, production system, runtime service, or private logs were accessed; no tests were executed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Historical reconstruction of all policy/configuration inputs and full price-tail coverage is unproven.

## FINDINGS FOR JEAN

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not freeze FIX-156 under the no-Production-touch rule yet.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Complete P01–P07/E03–E10; place the exact reviewed specification in the required canonical artifact; add the missing precedence oracle; and produce a class-by-class Replay-only file registry that excludes Production/shared source edits.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Require an explicit refusal path for every unavailable historical input and a sealed, cost-aware acceptance protocol to prevent selective exclusions and Replay/Production drift.

VERDICT: REJECTED
